Get all my workspaces
Authorizations:
MarketplaceKeyAuthApiKeyAuth
query Parameters
roles	
string
Enum: "WORKSPACE_ADMIN" "OWNER" "TEAM_MANAGER" "PROJECT_MANAGER"
Example: roles=WORKSPACE_ADMIN&roles=OWNER
If provided, you'll get a filtered list of workspaces where you have any of the specified roles. Owners are not counted as admins when filtering.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json
Array 
cakeOrganizationId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents the Cake organization identifier across the system.

costRate	
object (RateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents hourly rate object.

amount	
integer <int32>
Represents an amount as integer.

currency	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents a currency.

currencies	
Array of objects (CurrencyWithDefaultInfoDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents currency with default info object.

Array 
code	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents currency code.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents currency identifier across the system.

isDefault	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether curency should be set as default.

featureSubscriptionType	
FeaturePlan (object) (FeaturePlan)
Default: "##default"
Represents a feature plan type enum.

One of FeaturePlan
object FeaturePlan Recursive
features	
object (Feature)
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of features.

One of object

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
activeBillableHours	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether billable hours is active.

hourlyRate	
object (HourlyRateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an hourly rate object.

amount	
integer <int32>
Represents an amount as integer.

currency	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents a currency.

id	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents workspace identifier across the system.

imageUrl	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an image url.

memberships	
Array of objects (MembershipDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of membership objects.

Array 
costRate	
object (RateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents hourly rate object.

hourlyRate	
object (HourlyRateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an hourly rate object.

membershipStatus	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PENDING" "ACTIVE" "DECLINED" "INACTIVE" "ALL"
Represents a membership status enum.

membershipType	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "WORKSPACE" "PROJECT" "USERGROUP"
Represents membership type enum.

targetId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents target identifier across the system.

userId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents user identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents workspace name.

subdomain	
object (WorkspaceSubdomainDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents the workspace subdomain

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether subdomain is enabled on workspace

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents subdomain name

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
activeBillableHours	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether billable hours is active.

adminOnlyPages	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PROJECT" "TEAM" "REPORTS"
Represents a unique list of protected page enums.

automaticLock	
object (AutomaticLockDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an automatic lock object.

canSeeTimeSheet	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether timesheets are visible or not.

canSeeTracker	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether time trackers are visible or not.

currencyFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "CURRENCY_SPACE_VALUE" "VALUE_SPACE_CURRENCY" "CURRENCY_VALUE" "VALUE_CURRENCY"
Represents a clockify currency format enum.

defaultBillableProjects	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are billable by default.

durationFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "FULL" "COMPACT" "DECIMAL"
Represents a clockify duration format enum. Used to set Duration format instead of setting decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond.

entityCreationPermissions	
object (EntityCreationPermissionsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an entity creation permission object.

forceDescription	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether description are forced or not.

forceProjects	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are forced or not.

forceTags	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether tags are forced or not.

forceTasks	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether tasks are forced or not.

isProjectPublicByDefault	
boolean
lockTimeEntries	
string
Default: "##default"
lockTimeZone	
string
Default: "##default"
multiFactorEnabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether two-factor authentication is enabled or not.

numberFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "COMMA_PERIOD" "PERIOD_COMMA" "QUOTATION_MARK_PERIOD" "SPACE_COMMA"
Represents a clockify number format enum.

onlyAdminsCanChangeBillableStatus	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can change billable status.

onlyAdminsCreateProject	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can create projects.

onlyAdminsCreateTag	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can create tags.

onlyAdminsCreateTask	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can create task.

onlyAdminsSeeAllTimeEntries	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can see all time entries.

onlyAdminsSeeBillableRates	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether only admins can see billable rates.

onlyAdminsSeeDashboard	
boolean
Default: false

onlyAdminsSeePublicProjectsEntries	
boolean
Default: false

projectFavorites	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether project favorites are allowed.

projectGroupingLabel	
string
Default: "##default"

projectLabel	
string
Default: "##default"

boolean
Default: false

round	
object (RoundDto)

taskLabel	
string
Default: "##default"

boolean
Default: false

string
Default: "##default"

trackTimeDownToSecond	
boolean

Array of strings unique
Default: "##default"

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
activeBillableHours	
boolean
Default: false

integer <int32>

currency	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents a currency.

string
Default: "##default"
Represents workspace identifier across the system.

imageUrl	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents an image url.

Array of objects (MembershipDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents a list of membership objects.

object (RateDtoV1)

integer <int32>

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether subdomain is enabled on workspace

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents subdomain name

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
activeBillableHours	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether billable hours is active.

adminOnlyPages	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PROJECT" "TEAM" "REPORTS"
Represents a unique list of protected page enums.

automaticLock	
object (AutomaticLockDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an automatic lock object.

canSeeTimeSheet	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether timesheets are visible or not.

canSeeTracker	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether time trackers are visible or not.

currencyFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "CURRENCY_SPACE_VALUE" "VALUE_SPACE_CURRENCY" "CURRENCY_VALUE" "VALUE_CURRENCY"
Represents a clockify currency format enum.

defaultBillableProjects	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are billable by default.

durationFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "FULL" "COMPACT" "DECIMAL"
Represents a clockify duration format enum. Used to set Duration format instead of setting decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond.

entityCreationPermissions	
object (EntityCreationPermissionsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an entity creation permission object.

boolean

Indicates whether description are forced or not.

boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are forced or not.

boolean
Default: false

boolean
Default: false

boolean

Default: "##default"

Default: "##default"

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true

boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether billable hours is active.

put
/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/hourly-rate
https://api.clockify.me/api/v1/workspaces/{workspaceId}/hourly-rate
Request samples
Payload
Content type
application/json

Copy
{
"amount": 2000,
"currency": "USD",
"since": "2020-01-01T00:00:00Z"
}
Response samples
200
Content type
application/json

Copy
Expand allCollapse all
{
"cakeOrganizationId": "67d471fb56aa9668b7bfa295",
"costRate": "##default",
"currencies": "##default",
"featureSubscriptionType": "STANDARD_2021",
"features": [
"ADD_TIME_FOR_OTHERS",
"ADMIN_PANEL",
"ALERTS",
"APPROVAL"
],
"hourlyRate": "##default",
"id": "64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303",
"imageUrl": "https://www.url.com/imageurl-1234567890.jpg",
"memberships": "##default",
"name": "Cool Company",
"subdomain": "##default",
"workspaceSettings": "##default"
}
Add user to a workspace
You can add users to a workspace via API only if that workspace has a paid subscription. If the workspace has a paid subscription, you can add as many users as you want but you are limited by the number of paid user seats on that workspace.

Authorizations:
ApiKeyAuthAddonKeyAuth
path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 64a687e29ae1f428e7ebe303
Represents a workspace identifier across the system.

query Parameters
send-email
required
string
Default: "true"
Indicates whether to send an email when user is added to the workspace.

Request Body schema: application/json
required
email
required
string non-empty
Default: "##default"
Represents an email address of the user.

Responses
200 OK
Response Schema: application/json

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true

Array 
costRate	
object (RateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents hourly rate object.

hourlyRate	
object (HourlyRateDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an hourly rate object.

membershipStatus	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PENDING" "ACTIVE" "DECLINED" "INACTIVE" "ALL"
Represents a membership status enum.

membershipType	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "WORKSPACE" "PROJECT" "USERGROUP"
Represents membership type enum.

targetId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents target identifier across the system.

userId	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents user identifier across the system.

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents workspace name.

subdomain	
object (WorkspaceSubdomainDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents the workspace subdomain

enabled	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether subdomain is enabled on workspace

name	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents subdomain name

workspaceSettings	
object (WorkspaceSettingsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Workspace settings also include Time Duration Format settings.

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

2. Compact (h:mm) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = false,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
activeBillableHours	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether billable hours is active.

adminOnlyPages	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "PROJECT" "TEAM" "REPORTS"
Represents a unique list of protected page enums.

automaticLock	
object (AutomaticLockDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an automatic lock object.

canSeeTimeSheet	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether timesheets are visible or not.

canSeeTracker	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether time trackers are visible or not.

currencyFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "CURRENCY_SPACE_VALUE" "VALUE_SPACE_CURRENCY" "CURRENCY_VALUE" "VALUE_CURRENCY"
Represents a clockify currency format enum.

defaultBillableProjects	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are billable by default.

durationFormat	
string
Default: "##default"
Enum: "FULL" "COMPACT" "DECIMAL"
Represents a clockify duration format enum. Used to set Duration format instead of setting decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond.

entityCreationPermissions	
object (EntityCreationPermissionsDtoV1)
Default: "##default"
Represents an entity creation permission object.

forceDescription	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether description are forced or not.

boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether projects are forced or not.

forceTags	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether tags are forced or not.

forceTasks	
boolean
Default: false
Indicates whether tasks are forced or not.

isProjectPublicByDefault	
boolean
lockTimeEntries	
string
Default: "##default"
lockTimeZone	
string
Default: "##default"

boolean
Default: false

string
Default: "##default"

Represents a clockify number format enum.

path Parameters
workspaceId
required
string

userId
required
string
Default: "##default"
Example: 89b687e29ae1f428e7ebe912
Represents a user identifier across the system.

Request Body schema: application/json
required
amount
required
integer <int32> >= 0
Represents an hourly rate amount as integer.

since	
string
Default: "##default"
Represents a date and time in yyyy-MM-ddThh:mm:ssZ format.

200 OK

Setting Time Duration Format by changing the boolean fields decimalFormat and trackTimeDownToSecond is now deprecated.

Time Duration Format can be set by durationFormat enum field.

Three different Time Duration modes will still map the boolean fields:

1. Full (hh:mm:ss) -> decimalFormat = false, trackTimeDownToSecond = true,

3. Decimal (h:hh) -> decimalFormat = true, trackTimeDownToSecond = true
